STIL (full available entry and applicable directory notes):

> TITLE: Chase [from the movie Midnight Express]
>  ARTIST: Giorgio Moroder
> COMMENT: "Back then in 1991, the c64 was pretty much alive for me for I had no
>          money for buying an amiga... but in that time my ambitions and desires
>          for making music rose! Since having no sequencer or other audio gear
>          except my Roland M5, I started doing c64 SIDs using a program called
>          AMP. It allowed to compose in a tracker-like environment, and had a
>          nice interface for creating sounds. I tried to learn several techniques
>          by covering Hubbard's Commando, but my best SID was a cover of Moroder's
>          Midnight Express." (LMan)
